# Talent search: Beast Mastery, level 60

Generated by `sim/cmd/talent-search` against client 1.60.1.70790, engine 5f1b39450. Gear: the committed level-60 alliance bare-preset BiS set (night-elf), so only talents vary. 13 candidates generated, 13 screened at 300 iterations, the best 10 plus the guide re-simmed at 800 iterations (seed 7, the same for every sim). Probes: 200 iterations. Run time 8s.

**Verdict: deep Beast Mastery + 1 Endurance Training -> Improved Tracking beats the guide by +6.3 DPS (+1.4%), beyond the combined error ±0.9.** Guide 462.4 ± 0.7, best 468.7 ± 0.7.

**Counting only variants that keep every unmodeled guide talent:** deep Beast Mastery + 1 Endurance Training -> Improved Tracking, +6.3 DPS (±0.9 combined), beyond error.

Survival-only talents (the client's data gives them no damage, so an unmodeled one may be re-spent): Improved Concussive Shot, Improved Revive Pet, Survival Tactics.

- Guide (frontmatter 5420001505001251/00524510012/0 (client 1.60.1.70790)), re-read by talent id against 1.60.1.70790: `FS1:1.60.1.70790:hunter:night-elf:5420001505001251/00524510012/0:`
- Winner: `FS1:1.60.1.70790:hunter:night-elf:5420001505001251/0050550001/4:`
- Winner keeping every unmodeled guide talent: `FS1:1.60.1.70790:hunter:night-elf:5420001505001251/0050550001/4:`

## Engine gaps

Talents the final classification calls unmodeled that sit in this spec's own tree or that the guide takes - what the engine lane is actually missing:

- Improved Aspect of the Monkey (Beast Mastery, tier 1)
- Improved Revive Pet (Beast Mastery, tier 1)
- Pathfinding (Beast Mastery, tier 2)
- Bestial Swiftness (Beast Mastery, tier 2)
- Improved Mend Pet (Beast Mastery, tier 3)
- Spirit Bond (Beast Mastery, tier 4)
- Intimidation (Beast Mastery, tier 4)

## What the engine credits each talent with

Probed around the guide build: a talent the guide takes is removed, any other is added at max rank (legality ignored for the probe). Per-point DPS; *damage* means the probe moved DPS by more than 0.1% (every probe shares the base run's seed, so a talent the engine ignores reproduces it exactly). A talent the engine's code never reads is *unmodeled*: it sims as zero, and whatever it grants is either missing or given to every character regardless (the paladin engine registers Seal of Command unconditionally, for one).

## What the engine credits each talent with

Probed around the guide build: a talent the guide takes is removed, any other is added at max rank (legality ignored for the probe). Per-point DPS; *damage* means the probe moved DPS by more than 0.1% (every probe shares the base run's seed, so a talent the engine ignores reproduces it exactly). A talent the engine's code never reads is *unmodeled*: it sims as zero, and whatever it grants is either missing or given to every character regardless (the paladin engine registers Seal of Command unconditionally, for one).

## What the engine credits each talent with

Probed around the guide build: a talent the guide takes is removed, any other is added at max rank (legality ignored for the probe). Per-point DPS; *damage* means the probe moved DPS by more than 0.1% (every probe shares the base run's seed, so a talent the engine ignores reproduces it exactly). A talent the engine's code never reads is *unmodeled*: it sims as zero, and whatever it grants is either missing or given to every character regardless (the paladin engine registers Seal of Command unconditionally, for one).

## Engine gaps

Talents the final classification calls unmodeled that sit in this spec's own tree or that the guide takes - what the engine lane is actually missing:

- Hawk Eye (Marksmanship, tier 0)
- Improved Concussive Shot (Marksmanship, tier 0)
- Scatter Shot (Marksmanship, tier 4)

## What the engine credits each talent with

Probed around the guide build: a talent the guide takes is removed, any other is added at max rank (legality ignored for the probe). Per-point DPS; *damage* means the probe moved DPS by more than 0.1% (every probe shares the base run's seed, so a talent the engine ignores reproduces it exactly). A talent the engine's code never reads is *unmodeled*: it sims as zero, and whatever it grants is either missing or given to every character regardless (the paladin engine registers Seal of Command unconditionally, for one).

## Engine gaps

Talents the final classification calls unmodeled that sit in this spec's own tree or that the guide takes - what the engine lane is actually missing:

- Improved Hamstring (Arms, tier 5)
- Iron Will (Protection, tier 0)

## What the engine credits each talent with

Probed around the guide build: a talent the guide takes is removed, any other is added at max rank (legality ignored for the probe). Per-point DPS; *damage* means the probe moved DPS by more than 0.1% (every probe shares the base run's seed, so a talent the engine ignores reproduces it exactly). A talent the engine's code never reads is *unmodeled*: it sims as zero, and whatever it grants is either missing or given to every character regardless (the paladin engine registers Seal of Command unconditionally, for one).

Probes: 200 iterations. Run time 7s.

## What the engine credits each talent with

Probed around the guide build: a talent the guide takes is removed, any other is added at max rank (legality ignored for the probe). Per-point DPS; *damage* means the probe moved DPS by more than 0.1% (every probe shares the base run's seed, so a talent the engine ignores reproduces it exactly). A talent the engine's code never reads is *unmodeled*: it sims as zero, and whatever it grants is either missing or given to every character regardless (the paladin engine registers Seal of Command unconditionally, for one).

Talents the final classification calls unmodeled that sit in this spec's own tree or that the guide takes - what the engine lane is actually missing:

## What the engine credits each talent with

Probed around the guide build: a talent the guide takes is removed, any other is added at max rank (legality ignored for the probe). Per-point DPS; *damage* means the probe moved DPS by more than 0.1% (every probe shares the base run's seed, so a talent the engine ignores reproduces it exactly). A talent the engine's code never reads is *unmodeled*: it sims as zero, and whatever it grants is either missing or given to every character regardless (the paladin engine registers Seal of Command unconditionally, for one).

## What the engine credits each talent with

Probed around the guide build: a talent the guide takes is removed, any other is added at max rank (legality ignored for the probe). Per-point DPS; *damage* means the probe moved DPS by more than 0.1% (every probe shares the base run's seed, so a talent the engine ignores reproduces it exactly). A talent the engine's code never reads is *unmodeled*: it sims as zero, and whatever it grants is either missing or given to every character regardless (the paladin engine registers Seal of Command unconditionally, for one).

Talents the final classification calls unmodeled that sit in this spec's own tree or that the guide takes - what the engine lane is actually missing:

## What the engine credits each talent with

Probed around the guide build: a talent the guide takes is removed, any other is added at max rank (legality ignored for the probe). Per-point DPS; *damage* means the probe moved DPS by more than 0.1% (every probe shares the base run's seed, so a talent the engine ignores reproduces it exactly). A talent the engine's code never reads is *unmodeled*: it sims as zero, and whatever it grants is either missing or given to every character regardless (the paladin engine registers Seal of Command unconditionally, for one).

## Engine gaps

Talents the final classification calls unmodeled that sit in this spec's own tree or that the guide takes - what the engine lane is actually missing:

- Improved Hamstring (Arms, tier 5)

## What the engine credits each talent with

Probed around the guide build: a talent the guide takes is removed, any other is added at max rank (legality ignored for the probe). Per-point DPS; *damage* means the probe moved DPS by more than 0.1% (every probe shares the base run's seed, so a talent the engine ignores reproduces it exactly). A talent the engine's code never reads is *unmodeled*: it sims as zero, and whatever it grants is either missing or given to every character regardless (the paladin engine registers Seal of Command unconditionally, for one).
